					<!-- Trigger button -->
<button class="search-trigger" aria-label="Search">
  <svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
       stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
    <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"></circle>
    <line x1="23" y1="23" x2="16.5" y2="16.5"></line>
  </svg>
</button>

<!-- Modal -->
<div class="search-modal" aria-hidden="true">
  <div class="search-backdrop"></div>
  <div class="search-box" role="dialog" aria-label="Site search">
    <form class="search-form" method="get" action="/">
      <input id="site-search" type="search" name="s" placeholder="Search…" autocomplete="off"
             aria-autocomplete="list" aria-controls="search-suggest" aria-expanded="false" />
      <div class="search-spinner" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    </form>

    <!-- Suggestions (fixed height, no shift) -->
    <div id="search-suggest" class="search-suggest" role="listbox" aria-label="Search suggestions"></div>
  </div>
</div>

<style>
  /* Trigger (no hover background) */
  .search-trigger {
    -webkit-appearance: none; appearance: none;
    background: transparent !important; border: 0; padding: 0; margin: 0;
    cursor: pointer; color: inherit; line-height: 1; -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
  }
  .search-trigger:hover, .search-trigger:active, .search-trigger:focus { background: transparent !important; box-shadow: none !important; }
  .search-trigger:focus { outline: none; }
  .search-trigger:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid currentColor; outline-offset: 2px; }

  /* Modal */
  .search-modal { display:none; position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:9999; }
  .search-modal.active { display:block; }
  .search-backdrop { position:absolute; inset:0; background:rgba(0,0,0,.9); }
  .search-box { position:relative; top:50%; transform:translateY(-50%); max-width:600px; margin:0 auto; padding:0 20px; }

  /* Input + spinner */
  .search-form { position: relative; }
  .search-form input {
    width:100%; padding:14px 44px 14px 16px; font-size:18px; border:0; border-radius:6px;
  }
  .search-spinner {
    position:absolute; right:12px; top:50%; transform:translateY(-50%);
    width:18px; height:18px; border-radius:50%;
    border:2px solid rgba(0,0,0,.2); border-top-color: rgba(0,0,0,.7);
    display:none; animation: spin .7s linear infinite;
  }
  .search-form.loading .search-spinner { display:block; }
  @keyframes spin { to { transform: translateY(-50%) rotate(360deg); } }

  /* Suggestions: fixed height to prevent layout shift */
  .search-suggest {
    margin-top:8px; background:#fff; border-radius:8px; box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.25);
    overflow:auto; height: 260px; /* <-- fixed height */
    opacity: 0; pointer-events: none; transition: opacity .15s ease;
  }
  .search-suggest.is-open { opacity: 1; pointer-events: auto; }

  .search-suggest a, .search-suggest button {
    width:100%; display:block; text-align:left; padding:12px 14px; border:0; background:#fff;
    color:#111; text-decoration:none; cursor:pointer; line-height:1.35;
  }
  .search-suggest a:hover, .search-suggest a[aria-selected="true"],
  .search-suggest button:hover, .search-suggest button[aria-selected="true"] { background:#f2f2f2; }
  .search-suggest .suggest-meta { display:block; font-size:12px; color:#666; margin-top:2px; }

  /* Skeleton rows */
  .suggest-skeleton { padding:12px 14px; }
  .sbar { height:12px; width:60%; background:linear-gradient(90deg,#eee,#f6f6f6,#eee); animation: shimmer 1.2s infinite; border-radius:4px; }
  .smeta { margin-top:8px; height:10px; width:30%; background:linear-gradient(90deg,#eee,#f6f6f6,#eee); animation: shimmer 1.2s infinite; border-radius:4px; }
  @keyframes shimmer { 0% { background-position:-200px 0 } 100% { background-position:200px 0 } }

  @media (max-width: 480px){
    .search-box { padding: 0 14px; }
    .search-suggest { height: 220px; } /* slightly shorter on small screens */
  }
</style>

<script>
(function(){
  const trigger   = document.querySelector('.search-trigger');
  const modal     = document.querySelector('.search-modal');
  const backdrop  = document.querySelector('.search-backdrop');
  const form      = document.querySelector('.search-form');
  const input     = document.getElementById('site-search');
  const listbox   = document.getElementById('search-suggest');

  // track input modality for post-close focus
  let lastInputWasKeyboard = false;
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (['Tab','ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','Enter','Escape'].includes(e.key)) lastInputWasKeyboard = true;
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousedown', () => lastInputWasKeyboard = false);
  document.addEventListener('touchstart', () => lastInputWasKeyboard = false, {passive:true});

  // prevent layout shift when locking scroll
  function getScrollbarWidth(){ return window.innerWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth; }

  trigger.addEventListener('click', () => {
    modal.classList.add('active');
    const sw = getScrollbarWidth();
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    document.body.style.paddingRight = sw + 'px';
    requestAnimationFrame(()=> input.focus());
  });

  function closeModal(){
    modal.classList.remove('active');
    document.body.style.overflow = '';
    document.body.style.paddingRight = '';
    hideSuggest();
    if (lastInputWasKeyboard) trigger.focus(); else trigger.blur();
  }

  backdrop.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeModal(); });

  // ------- Suggestions with loading state + cache -------
  function getApiBase(){
    const link = document.querySelector('link[rel="https://api.w.org"]');
    if (link && link.href) return link.href;
    return '/wp-json/';
  }
  const API_BASE = getApiBase().replace(/\/+$/,'');
  const SEARCH_URL = API_BASE + '/wp/v2/search?per_page=8&subtype=any&_fields=title,url,subtype&search=';

  const cache = new Map();
  let aborter = null;
  let activeIndex = -1;

  const debounce = (fn, wait=150) => { let t; return (...a)=>{ clearTimeout(t); t=setTimeout(()=>fn(...a), wait); }; };

  input.addEventListener('input', debounce(onType, 150));
  input.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyNav);

  // Keep the container visible height at all times:
  // open: add .is-open; close/empty: remove content but keep height
  function onType(){
    const q = input.value.trim();
    input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');

    if (!q){
      // show an empty state but keep box open so height remains
      listbox.innerHTML = '';
      listbox.classList.add('is-open');
      return;
    }

    if (cache.has(q)){ renderSuggest(cache.get(q), q); return; }

    form.classList.add('loading');
    showSkeleton();

    if (aborter) aborter.abort();
    aborter = new AbortController();

    fetch(SEARCH_URL + encodeURIComponent(q), { signal: aborter.signal, headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' } })
      .then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : [])
      .then(items => { cache.set(q, items); renderSuggest(items, q); })
      .catch(()=>{}) // ignore abort/errs
      .finally(()=> form.classList.remove('loading'));
  }

  function showSkeleton(rows = 4){
    listbox.innerHTML = '';
    for (let i=0;i<rows;i++){
      const s = document.createElement('div');
      s.className = 'suggest-skeleton';
      s.innerHTML = '<div class="sbar"></div><div class="smeta"></div>';
      listbox.appendChild(s);
    }
    listbox.classList.add('is-open'); // visible (opacity), but height is always reserved
  }

  function renderSuggest(items, q){
    listbox.innerHTML = '';
    const results = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];

    if (results.length){
      results.forEach((it, idx) => {
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = it.url || '#';
        a.role = 'option';
        a.setAttribute('data-index', String(idx));
        a.innerHTML = escapeHtml(it.title) + (it.subtype ? `<span class="suggest-meta">${escapeHtml(it.subtype)}</span>` : '');
        a.addEventListener('click', () => closeModal());
        listbox.appendChild(a);
      });
    } else {
      const empty = document.createElement('div');
      empty.className = 'suggest-empty';
      empty.innerHTML = '<div class="suggest-skeleton" style="padding:14px">No results. Press Enter to search all.</div>';
      listbox.appendChild(empty);
    }

    // View all
    const viewAll = document.createElement('button');
    viewAll.type = 'button';
    viewAll.role = 'option';
    viewAll.className = 'view-all';
    viewAll.setAttribute('data-index', String(results.length));
    viewAll.textContent = `View all results for “${q}”`;
    viewAll.addEventListener('click', () => { window.location.href = `/?s=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`; });
    listbox.appendChild(viewAll);

    listbox.classList.add('is-open');
    input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    activeIndex = 0; highlight(activeIndex);
  }

  function hideSuggest(){
    listbox.classList.remove('is-open'); // fades out but height is still reserved until modal closes
    listbox.innerHTML = '';
    input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    activeIndex = -1;
  }

  function escapeHtml(s){ return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, m => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m])); }

  // Keyboard nav for suggestions
  function onKeyNav(e){
    const options = Array.from(listbox.querySelectorAll('[role="option"]'));
    if (!options.length) return;

    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown'){ e.preventDefault(); activeIndex = (activeIndex + 1) % options.length; highlight(activeIndex, options); }
    else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp'){ e.preventDefault(); activeIndex = (activeIndex - 1 + options.length) % options.length; highlight(activeIndex, options); }
    else if (e.key === 'Enter'){
      if (activeIndex >= 0){
        e.preventDefault();
        const el = options[activeIndex];
        if (el.tagName === 'A'){ window.location.href = el.href; }
        else { el.click(); }
        closeModal();
      }
    }
  }

  function highlight(idx, options = Array.from(listbox.querySelectorAll('[role="option"]'))){
    options.forEach((el, i) => el.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(i === idx)));
    if (idx >= 0 && options[idx].scrollIntoView) options[idx].scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
  }
})();
</script>
				</div>
					</div>
				</div>
					</div>
				</div>

					<div class="e-con-inner">
		<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-ae59739 e-flex e-con-boxed e-con e-child" data-id="ae59739" data-element_type="container">
					<div class="e-con-inner">
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-405370fd elementor-nav-menu__align-justify elementor-nav-menu--dropdown-tablet elementor-nav-menu__text-align-aside elementor-nav-menu--toggle elementor-nav-menu--burger elementor-widget elementor-widget-nav-menu" data-id="405370fd" data-element_type="widget" data-settings="{&quot;layout&quot;:&quot;horizontal&quot;,&quot;submenu_icon&quot;:{&quot;value&quot;:&quot;&lt;svg aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;e-font-icon-svg e-fas-caret-down\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 320 512\&quot; xmlns=\&quot;http:\/\/www.w3.org\/2000\/svg\&quot;&gt;&lt;path d=\&quot;M31.3 192h257.3c17.8 0 26.7 21.5 14.1 34.1L174.1 354.8c-7.8 7.8-20.5 7.8-28.3 0L17.2 226.1C4.6 213.5 13.5 192 31.3 192z\&quot;&gt;&lt;\/path&gt;&lt;\/svg&gt;&quot;,&quot;library&quot;:&quot;fa-solid&quot;},&quot;toggle&quot;:&quot;burger&quot;}" data-widget_type="nav-menu.default">
								<nav aria-label="Menu" class="elementor-nav-menu--main elementor-nav-menu__container elementor-nav-menu--layout-horizontal e--pointer-underline e--animation-fade">
				<ul id="menu-1-405370fd" class="elementor-nav-menu"><li class="menu-item menu-item-type-post_type menu-item-object-page menu-item-12843"><a href="https://www.thehrdirector.com/business-news/" class="elementor-item">News</a></li>

		<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-65d1460c e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child" data-id="65d1460c" data-element_type="container" data-settings="{&quot;background_background&quot;:&quot;classic&quot;}">

					<div class="e-con-inner">
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-3a449db0 elementor-widget elementor-widget-theme-post-content" data-id="3a449db0" data-element_type="widget" id="post-content-data" data-widget_type="theme-post-content.default">
					<p>
<strong>JOBS CUT AND SALES DECLINE</strong> 
</p>
<p>
British firms are suffering job cuts, a sharp decline in sales and a freeze on recruiting permanent staff, according to a survey of the UK’s leading business troubleshooters. There’s also been a severe tightening of company expenses.
</p>
<p>
More than 40% of the interim executives questioned by Alium Partners said their most recent client company was feeling the impact of the credit crunch. They also reported the economic downturn was curtailing expansion plans. 
</p>
<p>
Alium,  an independent provider of high-level interim executives, questioned 1,072 interims working at chief executive and director level &#8211; one fifth of whom were in manufacturing and 15% in financial services. The interims reported the downturn was manifesting itself through: 
</p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0cm" type="disc">
<li class="MsoNormal" style="margin: 0cm 0cm 0pt">Tightening on expenses &#8211; 49%</li>
<li class="MsoNormal" style="margin: 0cm 0cm 0pt">Downturn in sales &#8211; 42%</li>
<li class="MsoNormal" style="margin: 0cm 0cm 0pt">Recruitment freeze on permanent staff &#8211; 30%</li>
<li class="MsoNormal" style="margin: 0cm 0cm 0pt">Job cuts &#8211; 29%</li>
<li class="MsoNormal" style="margin: 0cm 0cm 0pt">Curtailing expansion plans – 21%</li>
<li class="MsoNormal" style="margin: 0cm 0cm 0pt">Shelving corporate social responsibility projects – 7%</li>
<li class="MsoNormal" style="margin: 0cm 0cm 0pt">Training cutbacks – 7%.</li>
</ul>
<p>
Nick Robeson, chief executive of Alium Partners, said: “Our survey provides a revealing glimpse into the current state of British commerce and industry because interim executives are able to view difficulties objectively. It’s interesting that most interims say a clear strategy (41%) and strong leadership (32%) are key factors in the companies weathering the current economic climate.” 
</p>
<p>
Most of the interims’ assignments involved transforming or turning around a company or division (57%). They cited ineffective management (42%) and inefficient operations (44%) as the biggest weakness holding back their client organisations, while 21% estimated the value they generated in their last role was worth between £1m and £5m. Asked what level of confidence their current employer had in the economy compared to 2007, 46% said less, while only five per cent said they were more confident.
</p>
<p>
 </p>

				const lazyloadRunObserver = () => {
					const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
					const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
						entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
							if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
								let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
								if( lazyloadBackground ) {
									lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
								}
								lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
							}
						});
					}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
					lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
						lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
					} );
				};
				const events = [
					'DOMContentLoaded',
					'elementor/lazyload/observe',
				];
				events.forEach( ( event ) => {
					document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
				} );
			</script>
